# 🐭 S.N.O.W. Golem Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The S.N.O.W. Golem Mouse is an Event Mouse found during the annual Great Winter Hunt event.

| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Event Mice (Great Winter Hunt) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |

| Cheese: | See current Great Winter Hunt location | Charm: | See current Great Winter Hunt location |
| Locations: | Great Winter Hunt | Loot: | See current Great Winter Hunt location |

| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| A fearsome snow golem who has been summoned by the Frozen Horde as the ultimate snowball assault weapon, the S.N.O.W. Golem (Snow-Neutralizing Obliterator of Winter) is a terrifying sight to behold.<br>This mouse has but one goal: To ruin the fun of every MouseHunter and destroy all the snow forts it can see. The King has sanctioned the area around his castle for construction of the thousands of snow forts required to defend against this mouse.<br>It will never leave, it will never stop, it will keep coming and coming until it can secure victory! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 516 |  |  |

## Finding Event Mice

Information on how to locate and capture the S.N.O.W. Golem Mouse can be found on the current Great Winter Hunt location page.

## History and Trivia

- 10 December 2013: The S.N.O.W. Golem Mouse was introduced as part of the Great Winter Hunt 2013 event.

> If a hunter had not constructed a Snow Fort yet, but had used the required number of Snowball Bocconcini, then a different mouse is attracted and a message is left in the Hunter's Journal:
The S.N.O.W. Golem Mouse has shown his face, but I cannot defeat him without a Snow Fort.
As soon as I have enough Snow Blocks, I must BUILD my Snow Fort if I want to stand a chance against this snowy monster!

- 13 December 2016: The points/gold for S.N.O.W. Golem Mouse were changed.

# 🐭 Saboteur Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Saboteur Mouse is a breed of mouse found in the Slushy Shoreline and Bombing Run zone of the Iceberg. They are known to drop Heating Oil as loot in the Slushy Shoreline, and Hunting Research, Satchel of Gold, Sticky Charms, and Wax Charms in the Iceberg.

| Saboteur Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Icewing's Invasion (Bomb Squad) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 6,800 | Gold: | 850 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Hydro | Other Requirements: | None |
| Cheese: | No Preference | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | IcebergSlushy Shoreline | Loot: | Frozen Sealed BottleHeating OilHunting Research (5,000)Satchel of Gold (2,000)Sticky CharmWax Charm |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| This muscular mouse stores tiny, mouse-sized barrels of TNT deep underneath the Iceberg. While they can be used to quickly expand the deep caverns of the Iceberg, they are also effective when breaching castle walls. Like, say... the castle walls in Gnawnia!Attacking this mouse head-on is sure to result in being blown back, but perhaps there is a way to override his explosive arsenal... |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 375 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

The Saboteur Mouse has no specific preference for any kind of cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

The Saboteur Mouse can be encountered in the Slushy Shoreline only when a hunter has Softserve Charms armed. In the Iceberg, catching a Saboteur Mouse with the Iceberg Boiler Base, Remote Detonator Base, or Ultimate Iceberg Base armed will provide an additional advance through the Iceberg.

## History and Trivia

- 14 May 2012: The Saboteur Mouse was released in Slushy Shoreline.
- Between 14 May 2012 and 24 May 2012: The Saboteur Mouse dropped Interlocking Hull Fragments.
- 1 June 2012: The Saboteur Mouse began to drop loot in the Iceberg.

| Whisked from the Dojo at great personal risk to the King’s servants, the Sacred Shrine mice spent most of their days before the gauntlet protecting their shrine from evil spirits and performing exorcisms for the folk in outlying regions. Their prayer slips burn the impure with the power of sacred flame, and form a ring of protection around them. One prayer slip, properly applied to a trap, can release the spring without a touch. |  |  |  |

Sacred Shrine Mouse is only attracted to Gauntlet Cheese Tier 6.

- 5 July 2010: The King granted permission to use the Gauntlet Potion Tier 6, allowing Hunters to access the Sacred Shrine Mouse.

| Samurai Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Followers of Furoma |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 5,000 | Gold: | 850 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Tactical | Other Requirements: | None |
| Cheese: | Standard CheeseMaki Cheese | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | DojoTraining Grounds | Loot: | Combat Research (1,250 points)Luscious LumberSatchel of Gold (500 gold) |
| Links |  |  |  |

| Years of intense training under brutal conditions have left this mouse truly disciplined and war-hardened. Little gets by their defense, and it's rare that you'll catch one unguarded. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 172 |  |  |

## Cheese and Charm Preference

### Cheese

The Samurai Mouse is attracted mainly to Standard Cheese in the Training Grounds and Dojo locations.

In the Dojo location, the Samurai Mouse will also be attracted when hunting with Maki Cheese.

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Samurai Mouse.

## Power Type Weaknesses

| VeryEffective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Tactical | Physical | None |

## Locations

Hunters can encounter the Samurai Mouse in the Training Grounds and Dojo locations.

## Loot

The Samurai Mouse drops the following loot:

- Training Grounds

> Combat Research (1,250 points)
Satchel of Gold (500 gold)

- Dojo

> Combat Research (1,250 points)
Satchel of Gold (500 gold)

The Samurai Mouse will drop Luscious Lumber in the Training Grounds location when Hunters are working the Zurreal Trap Research Assignment.
6 Luscious Lumber are required to construct the Zurreal's Folly weapon.

## History and Trivia

- 10 October 2008: The Samurai Mouse was released in the Training Grounds.
- 27 November 2008: The Samurai Mouse's habitat expanded to the Dojo.
- 11 December 2008: The Samurai Mouse began to drop Satchel of Gold (500 gold) as loot.
- 23 August 2011: The Samurai Mouse began to drop Luscious Lumber as loot in the Training Grounds for Hunters working a Zurreal Trap Research Assignment..

# 🐭 Sand Colossus Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Sand Colossus Mouse is a breed of mouse that can be found in the Sand Crypts, and is known to drop Aleth Essences, Ber Essences and Lunaria Petals as loot.

| Sand Colossus Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Sand Dunes Mice |  |  |  |

| Points: | 20,500 | Gold: | 1,500 |

| Cheese: | Graveblossom Camembert | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Sand Crypts | Loot: | Aleth EssenceBer EssenceLunaria Petal |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Centuries ago, an infamous hunter used a unique and terrible trap to capture the very souls of mice and confine them in gemstones. When the rest of the world found out, the hunter was banished and his name and the details of his trap were erased from history.In the intervening years, the mice have started breaking free from the gemstones and using the very sand around them to craft a monstrous form. Be careful hunting these mice down! They've already been caught once before and are twice as prepared! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 430 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

Sand Colossus mice have been found to be only attracted to Graveblossom Camembert.

## Hunting Strategy

The Sand Colossus Mouse is a member of the Sand Dunes mouse group. As such all power types except Shadow weapons are ineffective against it.

## History and Trivia

- Sand Colossus mice were released on 14 November 2012, as part of the Living Garden update.

Scarlet Revenger Mouse has a chance to drop Storm Cell if Rocket Boosters have been attached on the Hunter's Dirigible and the following loot depending on the buffs present:

| --- | --- |
| Sky Glass | Always drops Boosted by Sky Glass Formations and Loot Caches |
| Sky Ore | Always drops Boosted by Sky Ore Deposit and Loot Caches |

| Cloud Curd | Drops when a Cloud Curd Bonus is present Can be boosted by Loot Caches |

- Cyclone Stone
- Flawed Orb

The Scavenger Mouse is attracted to both Ancient Cheese and Radioactive Blue cheese.

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Scavenger Mouse.

| VeryEffective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Arcane | ShadowForgotten | None |

- 4 February 2009: The Scavenger Mouse was introduced with the release of the Catacombs location.
- 19 March 2009: The Scavenger Mouse began dropping Ancient Potion as loot.
- 12 January 2011: The Scavenger Mouse was found at Ronza's Traveling Shoppe where it dropped Rockforth cheese as loot.
- 16 April 2011: The Scavenger Mouse began dropping the Antique Blue Argyle Egg as loot in the Catacombs as part of the Spring Egg Hunt 2011 event.
- 4 April 2012: The Scavenger Mouse began dropping a bonus Scavenger Egg as loot as part of the Spring Egg Hunt 2012 event.
- The description for the Scavenger Mouse has changed since its release. The original description read as follows:

> The Catacombs are a dangerous place where mouse and hunter alike meet their fate. Scavenger mice sneak into the labyrinth that is the Catacombs collecting various bits of discarded cheese, gold and equipment. Scavengers are very protective of their findings and those who get in their way often find themselves at the wrong end of a shovel!

- 29 July 2015: The quantity of Scrap Metal dropped by Scavenger Mouse is increased.

Scrap Metal Monster mice are attracted to Magical Rancid Radioactive Blue at every spill level and to Rancid Radioactive Blue when spill level is for Lord/Lady and above.

## Hunting Strategy

Hydro type traps such as Oasis Water Node Trap or Phantasmic Oasis Trap are effective against the Scrap Metal Monster Mouse.

## Event Participation

- The Scrap Metal Monster Mouse drops the Toxic Spill Egg during the annual Spring Egg Hunt.

## History and Trivia

- Released on 21 January 2014 as part of the Toxic Spill content.

- 15 December 2010: TheScrooge Mouse dropped Heart of Ice as loot. The original notification about the re-release announced that the Scrooge Mouse dropped a Frozen Heart as loot; it was subsequently renamed Heart of Ice during a game update on the same day.

To pull itself away from the influence of the King, it has created its own sanctuary deep within an infinite tower. Here, tucked safely away from the King's scouts, it weaves a dark ritual...<br>Those strong enough to scale the tower will eventually meet one of the many shades of the Eclipse. It leaves one behind everytime it separates its reality into a new one, ducking behind the veil yet again.<br>Capturing a Shade will temporarily stabilize the Rift, syncing the timelines, allowing hunters to keep their tenuous grip on their position in the tower for just... a little... longer... |  |  |  |

### Cheese

The Shaman Mouse is attracted mainly to Standard Cheese.

In the Calm Clearing location, the Shaman Mouse will also be attracted when hunting with Cherry Cheese.
In the Great Gnarled Tree location, the Shaman Mouse will also be attracted when hunting with Gnarled Cheese.
In the Lagoon location, the Shaman Mouse will not be attracted when hunting with Gnarled Cheese or Wicked Gnarly Cheese.

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Shaman Mouse.

## Power Type Weaknesses

| VeryEffective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Tactical | PhysicalShadowArcaneHydroLaw | None |

## Locations

Hunters can encounter the Shaman Mouse in the Calm Clearing, Great Gnarled Tree, andLagoon locations.

## Loot

The Shaman Mouse drops Splintered Wood in the Calm Clearing, Great Gnarled Tree, andLagoon locations.

## History and Trivia

- 31 March 2009: The Shaman Mouse was released with the introduction of the Calm Clearing.
- 14 April 2011: The Shaman Mouse began dropping the Antique Green Plaid Egg as loot as part of the Spring Egg Hunt 2011 event.
- 25 June 2013: The Shaman Mouse gained a vulnerability to Law traps with the release of Varmint Valley.

# 🐭 Shorts-All-Year Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Shorts-All-Year Mouse is an Event Mouse found during the annual Great Winter Hunt event.

| Shorts-All-Year Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| This old, retired, grumpy gus of an engineer has put his yearly plans to travel to warmer climes for the winter on hold. He's usually on a westerly wagon to the Sandtail Desert by now, but the Festive Comet unfortunately landed right on top of his wagon before he boarded. Now, he's a bit stranded and, as you can imagine, a bit cold!<br>This mouse has meticulously planned his retirement around being just the right amount of warm all year; never too hot, and never, ever too cold. So much thought has gone into this lifestyle decision that he has changed his whole wardrobe to revolve around only wearing his favourite summer apparel: Shorts!<br>Stubborn as ever, he has put his engineering know-how (and most of his retirement savings) towards developing quite the sophisticated network of portable heaters to heat things up a bit! Nothing will get in this mouse's way when it comes to enjoying his retirement!<br>He is not letting a little snow ruin his plans for having his fun in the sun this winter, but the sudden rapid heating near the Festive Comet is threatening to thaw the Winter Hunt cheer too early this year and he must be stopped! |  |  |  |

## History and Trivia

- 16 March 2010: The Silth Mouse was released for the first time.
- 25 January 2011: The Silth Mouse began to drop Divine Orbs as loot.
- 22 March 2011: Catching the Silth Mouse became one requirement for gaining the Sandtail Call to Arms which allows travel to the Fiery Warpath. Hunters who had already caught a Silth Mouse did not have to catch another one.
- 14 April 2011: The Silth Mouse began dropping a bonus Silth Egg as loot as part of the annual Spring Egg Hunt event.
- 29 August 2012: The Silth Mouse began dropping Ancient Relics as loot.
- 29 July 2012: Catching the Silth Mouse stopped being a requirement for gaining the Sandtail Call to Arms.
- Originally the Silth Mouse dropped Plankrun's Journal Cover, but this was removed with the upgrade to MouseHunt 3.0.
- In the first month that the Silth Mouse was released, MouseHunt developers hosted a contest to determine the top three hunters who could catch the most of this breed. Prizes were awarded in the amounts of $50 of SUPER|brie+ (3,500 pieces), $25 of SUPER|brie+ (1,600 pieces), and $10 of SUPER|brie+ (500 pieces), respectively.

# 🐭 Silvertail Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Silvertail Mouse is a breed of mouse found in the Gnawnia and Valour regions. It is known to drop Simple Orb in each location and Packet of Flour at the Windmill..

| Silvertail Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Mountain Mice |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 1,200 | Gold: | 1,800 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Physical, Shadow, Tactical, Arcane, Forgotten, Hydro or Law | Other Requirements: | None |
| Cheese: | SUPER&#124;brie+ | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | HarbourKing's ArmsMountainTournament HallTown of GnawniaWindmill | Loot: | Packet of FlourSimple Orb |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Using its nearly impervious tail, the Silvertail mouse can solve problems that leave other mice scratching their heads. When confronting a trap, it simply wraps its namesake around the cheese, and ignores the sparks and bent mechanisms that follow. Extreme heat is one of its tail's few weaknesses, but hunters avoid melting the silver this way. Because of the tail's value as a lucky charm, an intact Silver Tail is worth a hefty reward from local merchants. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 237 |  |  |

## Cheese and Charm Preference

### Cheese

The Silvertail Mouse has been found to be especially attracted to SUPER|brie+ cheese, to the point that any other kind of cheese will simply not suffice.

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Silvertail Mouse.

Hunters may arm the Prospector's Charm in the Mountain location to improve the attraction rate of the Silvertail Mouse, Diamond Mouse, and Gold Mouse.

## Power Type Weaknesses

| VeryEffective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| None | All exceptDraconic, Parental and Rift | None |

## Loot

The Silvertail Mouse drops loot in the following locations:

- Harbour, King's Arms, Mountain, Tournament Hall, and Town of Gnawnia

> Simple Orb

- Windmill

> Packet of Flour, Simple Orb

## Event Participation

- Ronza - 28 June through 12 July 2016

> Location: Ronza's Traveling Shoppe 2016
Cheese Preference: SUPER|brie+
Loot: Rockforth Cheese, Simple Orb

- Ronza - 30 June through 7 July 2015

> Location: Ronza's Traveling Shoppe 2015
Cheese Preference: SUPER|brie+
Loot: Rockforth Cheese, Simple Orb

- MouseHunt's Sixth Birthday - 4-25 March 2014

> Location: Dance Hall
Cheese Preference: SUPER|brie+
Loot: Cupcake Colby cheese, Cupcake Colby Potion, Simple Orb

- MouseHunt's Fifth Birthday - 7-18 March 2013

> Location: Ronza's Traveling Shoppe
Cheese Preference: SUPER|brie+
Loot: Rockforth Cheese, Simple Orb

- Spring Egg Hunt 2011 - 14 April through 10 May 2011

> Location: Chocolate Factory

- MouseHunt's 3rd Birthday Celebration - 4-24 March 2011

> Location: Birthday Party
Cheese Preference: Cupcake Colby cheese
Loot: Simple Orb

## History and Trivia

- 30 June 2010: The Silvertail Mouse was released with the introduction of the Windmill.
- 12 January 2011: The Silvertail Mouse was found at Ronza's Traveling Shoppe where it dropped Attraction Charm and Rockforth cheese as loot.
- 25 January 2011: The Silvertail Mouse began to drop Simple Orb as loot.
- 25 June 2013: The Silvertail Mouse gained a vulnerability to Law power type traps with the release of Varmint Valley.
- 25 March 2014: The Silvertail Mouse gained a vulnerability to Rift power type traps with the release of the Rift Plane.
- 2 September 2014: The Silvertail Mouse began dropping Packet of Flour at the Windmill.
- 17 October 2018: The Silvertail Mouse changed mouse groups from Indigenous Mice to Mountain Mice.
- 5 October 2021: The Gold for Silvertail Mouse was increased as part of the Town of Gnawnia and Windmill gameplay updates.

The Sir Fleekio Mouse prefers Glowing Gruyere, but can be attracted with Standard Cheeses.

Forgotten weapons are very effective against the Sir Fleekio Mouse. Arcane weapons are of normal effectiveness.

### Cheese

| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |

| These magnificent creatures glide through the skies with incredible poise and grace. They are extraordinarily beautiful but suspiciously captivating. |  | Mouse ID#: 1052 |  |  |

## Cheese Preference

- On an Arcane Island or an Arcane Keep, the Sky Dancer Mouse is only attracted to Cloud Cheesecake.
- In an Arcane Cauldron, the Sky Dancer Mouse is only attracted to Extra Rich Cloud Cheesecake.

## Hunting Strategy

Only Arcane traps are effective against the Sky Dancer Mouse, all other power types are ineffective. The Sky Dancer Mouse can only be encountered on an Arcane Island, an Arcane Keep, or an Arcane Cauldron.

When found on a Low Altitude Island or a High Altitude Island, depending on the buffs present, the Sky Dancer Mouse can drop the following loot:

| --- | --- |
| Sky Glass | Always drops Boosted by Sky Glass Formations and Loot Caches |
| Sky Ore | Always drops Boosted by Sky Ore Deposit and Loot Caches |

| Corsair's Curd | Drops when a Pirate Den is present Can be boosted by Loot Caches |

## History and Trivia

- 08 September 2020: The Sky Dancer Mouse was introduced with the release of the Floating Islands.

| A fierce and powerful member of an ancient royal family that has reigned over the arcane islands for countless generations. The Sky Highborne Mouse is only attracted to Cloud Cheesecake.

## Hunting Strategy

Only Arcane traps are effective against the Sky Highborne Mouse, all other power types are ineffective. The Sky Highborne Mouse can only be encountered on an Arcane Keep.

| Item | Condition |

| Sky Ore | Always drops Boosted by Sky Ore Deposit and Loot Caches |

| Corsair's Curd | Drops when a Pirate Den is present Can be boosted by Loot Caches |

- 08 September 2020: The Sky Highborne Mouse was introduced with the release of the Floating Islands.

## Cheese and Charm Preference

### Cheese

The Smoldersnap Mouse is only attracted to Medium Queso, Hot Queso or Flamin' Queso.

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Smoldersnap Mouse.

## Hunting Strategy

Only Draconic traps are effective against the Smoldersnap Mouse, all other power types are ineffective. The Smoldersnap Mouse can only be encountered in Queso Geyser while either a Tiny Eruption, Small Eruption, Medium Eruption, Large Eruption or a Epic Eruption is triggered.

Dragonbane Charm, Super Dragonbane Charm, Extreme Dragonbane Charm, and Ultimate Dragonbane Charm provide a power bonus against the Smoldersnap Mouse.

## History and Trivia

- 30 April 2019: The Smoldersnap Mouse was introduced, with the release of the Queso Geyser area.

The Snowblind Mouse is a breed of mouse found in The Mad Depths zone of the Iceberg. It is known to drop Hunting Research, Satchel of Gold, Sticky Charms, and Wax Charms as loot.

| Snowblind Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Icewing's Invasion (Bergling) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 8,835 | Gold: | 850 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Hydro | Other Requirements: | None |
| Cheese: | No Preference | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Iceberg | Loot: | Hunting Research (5,000)Satchel of Gold (2,000)Sticky CharmWax Charm |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Having been caught by a stray ray of sunlight once too many, the Snowblind Mouse now dons protective gear every time he heads out. Unsatisfied with merely avoiding danger, however, he has upgraded his snow visor to display on-demand trap statistics that he uses to reformulate devious new cheese-stealing strategies on the fly. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 390 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

The Snowblind Mouse has no specific preference for any kind of cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

In the Iceberg, a Wax Charm will increase the distance of a successful catch, and a Sticky Charm will reduce the distance of a miss.

## History and Trivia

- 24 May 2012: The Snowblind Mouse was released as a part of the Iceberg.
- 1 June 2012: The Snowblind Mouse began to drop loot.

# 🐭 Snowblower Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Snowblower Mouse is an Event Mouse found during the annual Great Winter Hunt event.

| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Event Mice (Great Winter Hunt) |  |  |  |

| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| If you want to build a huge snow castle, sure, you could go at it all old-school with shovels. If you wanted to take all day, anyway.But if you want to build a huge snow castle in a couple hours, you call over this mouse and just watch him work! Walls rise in the same time it takes to brew a hot cocoa. Battlements are built while you track down a candy cane to use as a stir stick. And if you ask really nicely, he may clear your driveway afterward too.But you're going to have to share some of that cocoa. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 449 |  |  |

## Finding Event Mice

Information on how to locate and capture the Snowblower Mouse can be found on the current Great Winter Hunt location page.

## History and Trivia

- 10 December 2012: The Snowblower Mouse was released as part of the Great Winter Hunt 2012 event, but was locked and Hunters were unable to encounter this mouse.
- 21 December 2012: The Snowblower Mouse was unlocked and could now be encountered and caught by Hunters., but only after catching a Confused Courier with the Letter to Santa, Age 11.
- 13 December 2016: The points/gold for Snowblower Mouse were changed.

# 🐭 Snowflake Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Snowflake Mouse is an Event Mouse found during the annual Great Winter Hunt event.

| Snowflake Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Event Mice (Great Winter Hunt) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 5,000 | Gold: | 3,000 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | All types | Other Requirements: | See current Great Winter Hunt location |
| Cheese: | See current Great Winter Hunt location | Charm: | See current Great Winter Hunt location |
| Locations: | Great Winter Hunt | Loot: | See current Great Winter Hunt location |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Light as a feather, the elusive Snowflake mouse can be seen drifting with the strong winter wind, using it as a means of easy Kingdom-wide conveyance. In their travels, Snowflake Mice commonly pickup various knick knacks and odd ends they find laying in the winter snow. Their love of shiny objects can, however, be used to a hunter's advantage -- use a Winter Charm and a Snowflake Mouse may visit to investigate your trap. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 355 |  |  |

## Finding Event Mice

Information on how to locate and capture the Snowflake Mouse can be found on the current Great Winter Hunt location page.

## History and Trivia

- 2 December 2011: The Snowflake Mouse was painted live during an 'All about the art' Feedback Friday. Ideas and suggestions for how the mouse should look were provided by the audience at the time of painting.
- 7 December 2011: The Snowflake Mouse was released as part of the Great Winter Hunt 2011 event, with this description:

> Light as a feather, the elusive Snowflake mouse can be seen drifting with the strong winter wind, using it as a means of easy Kingdom-wide conveyance. In their travels, Snowflake Mice commonly pickup various knick knacks and odd ends they find laying in the winter snow. Their love of shiny objects can, however, be used to a hunter's advantage -- place a magical Winter Charm by your bait and a Snowflake Mouse may visit to investigate your trap.

- 21-24 December 2012: The Snowflake Mouse dropped double the normal Ice Coins.
- 10 December 2013: The Snowflake Mouse's description was changed for the Great Winter Hunt 2013, reflecting the change in requirement for any specific cheese. The previous description read:

> Light as a feather, the elusive Snowflake mouse can be seen drifting with the strong winter wind, using it as a means of easy Kingdom-wide conveyance. In their travels, Snowflake Mice commonly pickup various knick knacks and odd ends they find laying in the winter snow. Their love of shiny objects can, however, be used to a hunter's advantage -- use SUPER|brie+ or Festive Feta and a Snowflake Mouse may visit to investigate your trap. Better yet, place a magical Winter Charm next to your bait to find more Ice Coins upon capturing one!

- 15 December 2015: The description was changed to add the following text:

> While racing down the toboggan courses, use a Winter Charm to attract the Snowflake Mouse who drops Toboggan Nitro when caught!

- 13 December 2016: The points/gold for Snowflake Mouse were changed.

| Vowed to protect the royalty of Zokor, these solemnly sworn-in soldiers commit their lives to the cause. Countless generations of their family lineage grow up and take up the guard as part of their heritage.<br>Armed to the teeth and dressed in fine plate armour, these soldiers make a formidable frontline defence! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 785 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

The Solemn Soldier Mouse prefers Glowing Gruyere in both the Labyrinth and Zokor, but can be attracted with Standard Cheeses.

## Hunting Strategy

Forgotten weapons are very effective against the Solemn Soldier Mouse. Arcane weapons are of normal effectiveness.

- Labyrinth

> The Solemn Soldier Mouse can be found in Superior and Epic Fealty Hallways.

- Zokor

> The Solemn Soldier Mouse can be found in the Inner Fealty Temple and the Templar's Sanctum.

## Loot

- Zokor

> Labyrinth Theme Scrap III and Plates of Fealty

## History and Trivia

- 9 September 2015: Solemn Soldier Mouse was released as part of the Hollow Heights.

# 🐭 Soul Binder Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Soul Binder Mouse is the District Boss of the Dark Library in Zokor. It is known to drop Ancient Relic, Sacred Script, and Scholar Scrolls.

| Soul Binder Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Citizens of Zokor (Lost Scholar) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 350,000 | Gold: | 100,000 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Forgotten | Other Requirements: | Dark Library |
| Cheese: | Glowing GruyereStandard Cheese | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Zokor | Loot: | Ancient RelicSacred ScriptScholar ScrollClimbing Vine of Scholars |

| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Master of the Dark Library, enslaver of souls and an all-powerful wizard of the unknown. He believes himself to be a supreme ruler amongst all mice and is looked up to as one by his followers.<br>All mystics and mages of Zokor worship him. They follow his guidance and teachings in pursuit of becoming all-powerful like him. Unfortunately for them, the most powerful of his closest assistants become enslaved by him, feeding off their power and dooming them to eternal servitude. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 786 |  |  |

## Hunting Strategy

Soul Binder Mouse is available in Dark Library district in Zokor after 20 mice has been caught. It can only be caught once per trip there.

Soul Binder Mouse can be attracted with Standard Cheese and Glowing Gruyere.

Forgotten power type is very effective against the this mouse. Arcane is effective.

## Loot

- Ancient Relic
- Sacred Script
- Scholar Scroll
- Climbing Vine of Scholars

## History and Trivia

- 9 September 2015: Soul Binder Mouse was released as part of Zokor location.
- 29 August 2017: Soul Binder Mouse began dropping Climbing Vine of Scholars in preparation for the release of Moussu Picchu.

# 🐭 Speedy Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Speedy Mouse is a breed of mouse found in various locations in Gnawnia, and in King's Arms.

| Speedy Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Indigenous Mice (Misc.) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 950 | Gold: | 1,200 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Any type | Other Requirements: | None |
| Cheese: | Brie CheeseGouda CheeseSUPER&#124;brie+ | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | King's ArmsTournament HallTown of GnawniaWindmill | Loot: | Packet of Flour |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| To the naked eye, the Speedy Mouse is less of a mouse, and more of a brown blurry comet with a pink tail. There are very few case studies regarding their behavior, but those that exist are strange indeed. Take, for example, the testimony of one very disgruntled citizen: “Before I could call for help, those little varmints knocked me over, rifled through my pockets, bounced on muh belly and untied muh shoes.” Sometimes, even efficiency can be a bad thing. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 229 |  |  |

## Cheese and Charm Preference

### Cheese

The Speedy Mouse is attracted only to Brie Cheese, Gouda Cheese, and SUPER|brie+.

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Speedy Mouse.

## Power Type Effectiveness

| Very Effective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| None | All except Parental | None |

## Loot

The Speedy Mouse drops Packets of Flour in the Windmill location.

The Speedy Mouse is not know to drop any loot in the Town of Gnawnia, King's Arms, or Tournament Hall locations.

## History and Trivia

- 30 June 2010: The Speedy Mouse was released with the introduction of the Windmill location.
- 25 June 2013: The Speedy Mouse gained a vulnerability to Law power type traps, with the release of Varmint Valley.
- 19 September 2013: The Speedy Mouse gained a taste for Brie and Gouda cheeses during the Gnawnia population shift, although SUPER|brie+ still remains the most effective.
- 25 March 2014: The Speedy Mouse gained a vulnerability to Rift power type traps, with the introduction of the Rift Plane region.
- 2 September 2014: The Speedy Mouse began dropping Packets of Flour as loot in the Windmill.
- 5 October 2021: The Gold for Speedy Mouse was increased as part of the Town of Gnawnia and Windmill gameplay updates.

When found on a Low Altitude Island or a High Altitude Island, depending on the buffs present, the Spheric Diviner can drop the following loot:

| --- | --- |
| Sky Glass | Always drops Boosted by Sky Glass Formations and Loot Caches |
| Sky Ore | Always drops Boosted by Sky Ore Deposit and Loot Caches |
| Cloud Curd | Drops when a Cloud Curd Bonus is present Can be boosted by Loot Caches |
| Corsair's Curd | Drops when a Pirate Den is present Can be boosted by Loot Caches |

## History and Trivia

| Spelunking in a nearby cave, this happy explorer of a mouse had no idea as to what had just happened outside. On his way out he noticed that the cave fungi were glowing exceptionally bright. Irradiated by toxic fungus, this spelunker now glows in the dark, making his exploring much easier! Also, as an added bonus, he doesn't have to go too far to find new and rare mushrooms! I don't think he could be any happier! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 539 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

Spore mice are attracted to Magical Rancid Radioactive Blue at every spill level and to Rancid Radioactive Blue when spill level is for Hero to Duke/Duchess.

## Hunting Strategy

Hydro type traps such as Oasis Water Node Trap or Phantasmic Oasis Trap are effective against the Spore Mouse.

## Event Participation

- The Spore Mouse drops the Toxic Spill Egg during the annual Spring Egg Hunt.

## History and Trivia

- Released on 21 January 2014 as part of the Toxic Spill content.

| Spotted mice enjoy the fresh air and solitude of long hikes in the countryside, and because they never stay in one place for long, they are rather unsure of themselves when it comes to making friends. These mice are ideal targets for beginning MouseHunters because, while they are fairly smart and physically fit, they lack the teamwork skills that other mice use to stay out of trouble. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 241 |  |  |

## Cheese and Charm Preference

### Cheese

The Spotted Mouse is attracted only to Standard Cheese.

In the Harbour location, the Spotted Mouse will not be encountered when hunting with White Cheddar Cheese.
In the Meadow location, the Spotted Mouse will not be encountered when hunting with Grilled Cheese.

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Spotted Mouse.

## Power Type Effectiveness

| Very Effective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| None | All except Parental | None |

## Loot

The Spotted Mouse drops Marble Cheese and Satchel of Gold (500) in the Windmill location.

The Spotted Mouse is not known to drop any loot in the Harbour or King's Arms locations.

## History and Trivia

- 13 July 2010 : The Spotted Mouse was introduced.
- 25 June 2013: The Spotted Mouse gained a vulnerability to Law power type traps with the release of Varmint Valley.
- 25 March 2014: The Spotted Mouse gained a vulnerability to Rift power type traps with the introduction of the Rift Plane.
- 25 March 2014: The Supernatural Mouse was introduced as the Rift counterpart of the Spotted Mouse.

# 🐭 Spring Familiar Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Spring Familiar Mouse is a breed of mouse found in the Rodentia region. It can be attracted and caught only during the Spring Season.

| Spring Familiar Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Seasonal Soldiers (Spring) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 9,072 | Gold: | 760 |

| Clad in churning billows of cloud, the Spring Familiar brings a concentrated electrical storm with it wherever it goes. When this mouse enters the area, take cover- it may look serene, but the lightning bolts shooting out of its skirt will fry any trap mechanism in its path. Like a slow-moving thunderhead, the Spring Familiar is never in a hurry. It knows that it can take its time pilfering and eating cheese, because hunters fear its shocking defenses. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 264 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

Spring Familiar mice have been found especially attracted to SUPER|Brie+ cheese, to the point that any other kind of cheese will simply not suffice.

## Hunting Strategy

Strong Physical weapons are recommended in order to catch this mouse. Alternatively, hunters may equip their best Tactical trap.

## History and Trivia

- Spring Familiar Mice were introduced on 17 August 2010 with the release of the Seasonal Garden location.
- Spring Familiar Mice began dropping Sandtail Call to Arms on 22 March 2011 with the release of the Fiery Warpath location.
- On 29 July 2015 they stopped dropping the Sandtail Call to Arms as loot with the introduction of the Adventure Book.

Only Forgotten traps are effective against the Spry Sky Explorer Mouse, all other power types are ineffective. The Spry Sky Explorer Mouse can only be encountered on a Forgotten Island or a Forgotten Fortress.

When found on a Low Altitude Island or a High Altitude Island, depending on the buffs present, the Spry Sky Explorer Mouse can drop the following loot:

| Sky Glass | Always drops Boosted by Sky Glass Formations and Loot Caches |
| Sky Ore | Always drops Boosted by Sky Ore Deposit and Loot Caches |
| Cloud Curd | Drops when a Cloud Curd Bonus is present Can be boosted by Loot Caches |
| Corsair's Curd | Drops when a Pirate Den is present Can be boosted by Loot Caches |

## History and Trivia

- 08 September 2020: The Spry Sky Explorer Mouse was introduced with the release of the Floating Islands.

Each one specializes in a particular art of thievery: a master of disguise, a maven of sleight of hand, and a professional tool man. They can quickly, but clumsily, shift positions depending on the requirements of each heist. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 1064 |  |  |

## Cheese Preference

The Stack of Thieves is primarily attracted to Cloud Cheesecake, however it also can be attracted to Standard Cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

Only Law traps are effective against the Stack of Thieves, all other power types are ineffective. The Stack of Thieves can only be encountered on a Law Island.

## Loot

### Floating Islands's Island Based Loot

When found on a Low Altitude Island or a High Altitude Island, depending on the buffs present, the Stack of Thieves can drop the following loot:

| Item | Condition |
| --- | --- |
| Sky Glass | Always drops Boosted by Sky Glass Formations and Loot Caches |
| Sky Ore | Always drops Boosted by Sky Ore Deposit and Loot Caches |
| Cloud Curd | Drops when a Cloud Curd Bonus is present Can be boosted by Loot Caches |
| Corsair's Curd | Drops when a Pirate Den is present Can be boosted by Loot Caches |

## History and Trivia

- 08 September 2020: The Stack of Thieves was introduced with the release of the Floating Islands.

| Cracking his whip and hollering for everyone and everything to get out of his way, this mouse can be heard a long way off. Fast is never fast enough with this guy. His only desire is to travel at neck-break speeds. Unfortunately, at high-velocity speeds, stagecoaches simply vibrate violently and fall apart, flinging their passengers across the dusty wasteland. This is his seventh stagecoach this year and it is becoming increasingly difficult to find willing passengers...<br>This mouse can be seen zipping along the horizon with a large dust cloud and the 'Travelling Terror' gang in tow. |  |  |  |

## Cheese and Charm Preference

### Cheese

The Steam Sailor Mouse is only attracted to Mild Queso, Medium Queso, Hot Queso or Flamin' Queso.

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Steam Sailor Mouse.

## Hunting Strategy

Catching a Steam Sailor Mouse while building pressure in Queso Geyser increases the amount of pressure by 5 kPa.

Only Draconic traps are effective against the Steam Sailor Mouse, all other power types are ineffective. The Steam Sailor Mouse can only be encountered while building pressure in Queso Geyser, after a cork has been constructed.

Dragonbane Charm, Super Dragonbane Charm, Extreme Dragonbane Charm, and Ultimate Dragonbane Charm provide a power bonus against the Steam Sailor Mouse.

## History and Trivia

- 30 April 2019: The Steam Sailor Mouse was introduced, with the release of the Queso Geyser area.

| As the name suggests, the skin of this mouse is strong as steel. Less common than simple colored mice, the steel mouse is significantly harder to catch. |  |  |  |

## Cheese and Charm Preference

### Cheese

The Steel Mouse is attracted to Standard Cheese.

In the Mountain location, the Steel Mouse is not attracted to Chedd-Ore Cheese or Abominable Asiago Cheese.
In the Bazaar and Town of Gnawnia locations, the Steel Mouse is also attracted to Gilded Cheese.

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Steel Mouse.

## Power Type Effectiveness

| Very Effective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| None | All except Parental | None |

## Loot

The Steel Mouse drops Marble Cheese and Satchel of Gold (500) in the Meadow.

The Steel Mouse has a chance to drop Scrap Metal in all locations but if captured with the Digby Drillbot or the Limited Edition Chrome DrillBot it drops Scrap Metal every time.

## History and Trivia

- 10 August 2010: The Steel Mouse started to drop Scrap Metal as loot when captured using the Digby DrillBot or the Chrome DrillBot.
- 15 December 2010: The Steel Mouse began dropping Seasoned Gouda Potions as part of the Great Winter Hunt 2010 event.
- 25 June 2013: The Steel Mouse gained a vulnerability to Law power type traps with the release of Varmint Valley.
- 25 March 2014: The Steel Mouse gained a vulnerability to Rift power type traps with the introduction of the Rift Plane.
- 25 March 2014: The Spiritual Steel Mouse was introduced as the Rift counterpart of the Steel Mouse.
- 14 October 2014: The Steel Mouse was given new art. The costumed version appeared during the Halloween 2014 event and the new uncostumed version appeared when the event ended on 4 November 2014.
- 17 October 2018: The Steel Mouse was removed from the Laboratory.
- 22 March 2022: The Steel Mouse got a chance to drop Scrap Metal in every location it is available at irrespective of the trap.

# 🐭 Stoutgear Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Stoutgear Mouse is a breed of mouse found in the Gnawnian Express Station. It can only be attracted and caught during the Raider River phase of a train journey. They are known to drop Copper Beads and Raider-B-Gone Mouse Repellents as loot.

| Stoutgear Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Train Robbers (Automice) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 7,000 | Gold: | 8,500 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Law | Other Requirements: | Raider River Stage |
| Cheese: | No Preference | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Gnawnian Express Station | Loot: | Copper BeadRaider-B-Gone Mouse Repellent |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Heavy, thick and made out of the strongest of iron, the stout Stoutgear Mouse is the pillar of raiding technology.<br>Though a little rusty, these mice are built strong to last long! Stoutgear mice are used as the main holding force in most raids, weakening the forces protecting the train to clear a path for the professionals. Once they get a foothold, they become a mechanical force with which to reckon! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 504 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

Stoutgear mice have no specific preference for any kind of cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

Law type traps such as S.L.A.C. are very effective against the Stoutgear Mouse. While an Automice encounter is guaranteed during a Trap Check, it is also possible to attract this mouse during normal hunting.

## Event Participation

- The Stoutgear Mouse drops the Gnawnian Express Egg during the annual Spring Egg Hunt.

## History and Trivia

- Released on 26 September 2013 as part of the Gnawnian Express Station content.

# 🐭 Stowaway Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Stowaway Mouse is a breed of mouse found in the Gnawnian Express Station. They are known to drop Fool's Gold.

| Stowaway Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Train Robbers (Passenger) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 4,000 | Gold: | 2,000 |

| Cheese: | SUPER&#124;brie+ | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Gnawnian Express Station | Loot: | Fool's Gold |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| He loves to ride the rails! Any chance he gets, he will sneak onto the next train to leave the station without a moment's notice! Unfortunately, this train addiction of his often leaves him too broke for train fare, which is why he must hitch a ride with the boxes in the back.Although he has easy access to the Royal Supply Crates, he'd rather not disturb them for fear his hiding spot will be discovered! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 505 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

While Stowaway mice can be attracted by standard cheese, they have a distinct preference for SUPER|brie+.

## Hunting Strategy

Law type traps such as S.L.A.C. are very effective against Stowaway Mouse.

## Event Participation

- The Stowaway Mouse drops the Gnawnian Express Egg during the annual Spring Egg Hunt.

## Event Participation

- MouseHunt's Ninth Birthday - 1 - 15 March 2017

> Location: Birthday Party Zone

- MouseHunt's Eighth Birthday - 3 - 21 March 2016

> Location: Birthday Party Zone
Cheese Preference: Cupcake Colby, Extra Sweet Cupcake Colby
Attraction: Must have a Year Six Order open
Loot: Blue Birthday Cupcake, Copper Bead, Iron Pellet, Tin Scrap

- Great Winter Hunt 2015 - 15 December 2015 through 12 January 2016

> Location: Extreme Toboggan Challenge -- Varmint Valley Course

- MouseHunt's Seventh Birthday - 5 - 19 March 2015

> Location: Birthday Party Zone
Cheese Preference: Cupcake Colby
Attraction: Must have a Year Six Order open
Loot: Blue Birthday Cupcake, Copper Bead, Iron Pellet, Tin Scrap

## History and Trivia

- Released on 26 September 2013 as part of the Gnawnian Express Station content.

# 🐭 Stratocaster Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Stratocaster Mouse is a breed of mouse found on the Floating Islands, on a Shadow Island or a Shadow Stronghold.

| Stratocaster Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Floating Islanders (Shadow Overcaster) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 102,000 | Gold: | 10,250 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Shadow | Other Requirements: | Shadow IslandShadow Sanctuary |
| Cheese: | Cloud Cheesecake | Charm: |  |
| Locations: | Floating Islands | Loot: | Sky OreSky Glass |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| She is considered the rock star of the shadow-controlled islands and she's got an attitude to match the moniker. She is rumoured to be so powerful, with a disposition so volatile, that she could throw a piece of the moon at you for merely getting in her way. These gifted few have earned the right to wield the powerful crescent sceptre and read from the mystical book of skyshadows. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 1065 |  |  |

## Cheese Preference

The Stratocaster Mouse is only attracted to Cloud Cheesecake.

## Hunting Strategy

Only Shadow traps are effective against the Stratocaster Mouse, all other power types are ineffective. The Stratocaster Mouse can only be encountered on a Shadow Island or a Shadow Sanctuary.

## Loot

### Floating Islands's Island Based Loot

When found on a Low Altitude Island or a High Altitude Island, depending on the buffs present, the Stratocaster Mouse can drop the following loot:

| Item | Condition |
| --- | --- |
| Sky Glass | Always drops Boosted by Sky Glass Formations and Loot Caches |
| Sky Ore | Always drops Boosted by Sky Ore Deposit and Loot Caches |
| Cloud Curd | Drops when a Cloud Curd Bonus is present Can be boosted by Loot Caches |
| Corsair's Curd | Drops when a Pirate Den is present Can be boosted by Loot Caches |

## History and Trivia

- 08 September 2020: The Stratocaster Mouse was introduced with the release of the Floating Islands.

# 🐭 Strawberry Hotcakes Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Strawberry Hotcakes Mouse is a breed of mouse that can be found in the Living Garden, and is known to drop Ber Essences and Dewthief Petals as loot.

| Strawberry Hotcakes Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Living Garden Mice |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 14,500 | Gold: | 1,950 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Hydro | Other Requirements: | None |
| Cheese: | Standard Cheese | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Living Garden | Loot: | Ber EssenceDewthief Petal |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| This little firebrand is simultaneously the most dangerous and most helpful mouse in the Living Garden. Threatening her friends? A FLOOD OF HOT SAUCE. Food too bland? A FLOOD OF HOT SAUCE.Her peppergun has incredible range, and the pepper reservoir on her back seems to never run dry, so don't try to outrange or outlast her. At best you can hope to surprise her, but act quickly because when surprised she unleashes A FLOOD OF HOT SAUCE. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 437 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

The Strawberry Hotcakes Mouse is attracted to Standard Cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

The Strawberry Hotcakes Mouse is a member of the Living Garden mouse group. As such all power types except Hydro weapons are ineffective against it.

## History and Trivia

- 14 November 2012: The Strawberry Hotcakes Mouse was released as a part of the Living Garden update.
- The name and clothing of the Strawberry Hotcakes Mouse are a reference to the Strawberry Shortcake character.

# 🐭 Stuck Snowball Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Stuck Snowball Mouse is an Event Mouse found during the annual Great Winter Hunt event.

Each catch of a Stuck Snowball Mouse increases the points awarded by 500 and the gold awarded by 350 (the second catch is 2,000 points and 650 gold, the third catch is 2,500 points and 1,000 gold, and so on). This is capped at 51,500 points and 36,000 gold.

| Stuck Snowball Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Event Mice (Great Winter Hunt) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 1,500+ | Gold: | 300+ |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | All types | Other Requirements: | See current Great Winter Hunt location |
| Cheese: | See current Great Winter Hunt location | Charm: | See current Great Winter Hunt location |
| Locations: | Great Winter Hunt | Loot: | See current Great Winter Hunt location |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| This slick little guy has slipped and got himself stuck in snow! What's worse? He can't stop! Rolling and gaining momentum throughout the snowy hills, not one trap has yet been fast enough to catch and stop this sailing snowball!How big will he get? Will he ever be stopped? |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 682 |  |  |

## Finding Event Mice

Information on how to locate and capture the Stuck Snowball Mouse can be found on the current Great Winter Hunt location page.

## History and Trivia

- 9 December 2014: The Stuck Snowball Mouse was introduced as part of the Great Winter Hunt 2014 event.

> Every catch of a Stuck Snowball Mouse causes an additional message to be left in the Hunter's Journal:
I just caught a Stuck Snowball Mouse for the X time! Poor guy keeps rolling though, getting bigger and bigger - pretty soon I should even get more loot from him!
I look forward to the next time I catch him!
Before the 51st catch, the message changes slightly every multiple of five catches:
I just caught a Stuck Snowball Mouse for the X time! He's definitely much bigger now. The next time I catch him, I should be able to get more stuff!

# 🐭 Student of the Cheese Belt Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

| Student of the Cheese Belt Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Followers of Furoma |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 6,250 | Gold: | 450 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Tactical | Other Requirements: | None |
| Cheese: | Maki CheeseSUPER&#124;brie+ | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Dojo | Loot: | Plankrun's Dojo NotesSatchel of Gold (750 gold)Token of the Cheese Belt |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Students of the Cheese Belt may look peaceful and docile, but don't let them catch you off guard. Because they are fueled by their limitless hunger, a trap will hardly be able to get between this mouse and your cheese! The masters of these students apparently have learned to control their cravings but tend to have a rough time refining the young students to their ways. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 196 |  |  |

## Cheese and Charm Preference

### Cheese

Maki Cheese has an attraction rate for the Student of the Cheese Belt Mouse of about twice that of SUPER|brie+ and four times that of Standard Cheeses.

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Student of the Cheese Belt Mouse.

## Power Type Weaknesses

| VeryEffective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Tactical | Physical | None |

## Locations

Hunters can encounter the Student of the Cheese Belt Mouse only in the Dojo location.

## Loot

The Student of the Cheese Belt Mouse drops the following loot in the Dojo location:

- Plankrun's Dojo Notes
- Satchel of Gold (750 gold)
- Token of the Cheese Belt

## History and Trivia

- 26 November 2008: The Student of the Cheese Belt Mouse is released.
- 27 November 2008: The Student of the Cheese Belt Mouse begins dropping the Token of the Cheese Belt as loot.
- 16 April 2011: The Student of the Cheese Belt Mouse began dropping the Antique Blue Argyle Egg as loot as part of the Spring Egg Hunt 2011 event.
- 14 April 2011: The Student of the Cheese Belt Mouse began dropping a bonus Dojo Student Egg as loot during the Spring Egg Hunt 2011 event.
- The description for the Student of the Cheese Belt Mouse has changed since its release. The original description read as follows:

> Students of the Cheese Belt may look peaceful and docile but don't let that catch you off guard. They are fueled by their hunger which is limitless, a trap will hardly be able to get between this mouse and your cheese! The masters of these students apparently have learned to control their cravings but tend to have a rough time refining the young students to their ways.

| Mouse Group: Floating Islanders (Sky Pirate) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 87,510 | Gold: | 21,000 |

| This charismatic character is smooth, sultry, and the definition of debonair. Be wary of him charming his way onto your ship because this suave sailor is out to steal more than just your loot. Before you know it, he'll have disappeared into the clouds, leaving you with an empty hull and a broken heart. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 1066 |  |  |

## Cheese Preference

The Suave Pirate Mouse is only attracted to Sky Pirate Swiss Cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

All powertypes except Rift and Parental are effective against the Suave Pirate Mouse. The Suave Pirate Mouse can only be encountered on an island with one or multiple Sky Pirate Den present and active.

## Loot

### Floating Islands's Sky Pirate Loot

Suave Pirate Mouse has a chance to drop Storm Cell if Rocket Boosters have been attached on the Hunter's Dirigible and the following loot depending on the buffs present:

| Item | Condition |
| --- | --- |
| Sky Glass | Always drops Boosted by Sky Glass Formations and Loot Caches |
| Sky Ore | Always drops Boosted by Sky Ore Deposit and Loot Caches |
| Sky Pirate Seal | Always Drops The amount a mouse drops depends on the mouse caught. |
| Cloud Curd | Drops when a Cloud Curd Bonus is present Can be boosted by Loot Caches |

Additionally, the Suave Pirate Mouse can drop the following loots as well:

- Bottled Wind
- Cyclone Stone

## History and Trivia

- 08 September 2020: The Suave Pirate Mouse was introduced with the release of the Floating Islands.

# 🐭 Subterranean Mouse

| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Digby Dirt Dwellers |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 11,000 | Gold: | 5,800 |

| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| After being run out of their laboratory, the scientists of Digby took refuge deep in the earth, hoping to wait out the mouse invasion. Shortly after digging in for the long haul, they found that the mice had taken over everything, including underground, which forced them to bring their drill city back to the surface. Now, by using their experience below ground, and a special irradiated crystal cheese, the cleverest hunters have been able to attract these deep digging mice. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 199 |  |  |

## Cheese and Charm Preference

### Cheese

The Subterranean Mouse is attracted only to Limelight Cheese.

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Subterranean Mouse.

Arming the Mining Charm provides a power boost against Digby Dirt Dwellers.

## Power Type Weaknesses

| VeryEffective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| None | PhysicalShadowTacticalArcane | None |

## Locations

Hunters can encounter the Subterranean Mouse only in the Town of Digby.

## Loot

The Subterranean Mouse drops Living Shards as loot.

## History and Trivia

- 24 March 2010: The Subterranean Mouse is released for the first time.

- 7 March 2013: The Super FighterBot MegaSupreme could not be encountered until a Hunter obtained and armed the Ultra MegaMouser MechaBot Trap from Ronza's Traveling Shoppe Trapsmith.

The Supply Hoarder Mouse is a breed of mouse found in the Gnawnian Express Station. It can only be attracted and caught during the Supply Depot phase during a Supply Rush. They are known to drop Iron Pellets and Royal Supply Crates as loot.

| Supply Hoarder Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Train Robbers (Depot Worker) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 8,000 | Gold: | 12,000 |

| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| These mice work for the Warehouse Manager Mouse, aiding him in severing the flow of Royal Supply Crates to the Gnawnian Express so that they can get their paws on the goodies inside.<br>They carry up to twice as many supplies as the Warehouse Manager and are a valuable catch, so be sure to stick around during a Supply Rush!<br>They only appear when a hunter has caught a Warehouse Manager Mouse and a Supply Rush has been triggered. Use Supply Schedule Charms for a chance to attract a Warehouse Manager Mouse and call these mice out of hiding! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 506 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

Supply Hoarder mice have no specific preference for any kind of cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

Law type traps such as S.L.A.C. are very effective against the Supply Hoarder Mouse. This mouse can only be caught during a Supply Rush, which requires the use of a Supply Schedule Charm to first catch a Warehouse Manager Mouse. However, as any other hunter in the Hunting Team riding the same train can trigger the supply rush, it is possible to catch this mouse without using any charms. During a Supply Rush, the Supply Schedule Charm provides no additional advantage, and use of a different charm may make it easier to catch a Supply Hoarder.

## Event Participation

- The Supply Hoarder Mouse drops the Gnawnian Express Egg during the annual Spring Egg Hunt.

## History and Trivia

- Released on 26 September 2013 as part of the Gnawnian Express Station content.

The Swabbie Mouse has no specific preference for any kind of cheese. It is known to have an aversion to SUPER|brie+.

## History and Trivia

- 23 June 2009: The Swabbie Mouse was released.
- 12 January 2011: The Swabbie Mouse could be found at Ronza's Traveling Shoppe where it dropped Power Charm as loot.
- The description for the Swabbie Mouse has changed since its release. The original description read as follows:

> Forced to cleanup after their shipmates, the Swabbie mouse is an under-appreciated pirate wannabe. This poor, hapless servant faces daily humiliation from his fellow shipmates.

| Vanilla harvesters live in fear of Pygmy Mice, who terrorize anyone who tries to get near their beloved vanilla vines on the far side of the Jungle of Dread. Normally, the Pygmy Mice’s rage is confined to their territorial groves, but, like most mice, the Pygmy Swarms prefer that their favorite food be delivered in cheese form. ## Preferred Cheese

The Swarm of Pygmy Mice have been found to be especially attracted to Gouda, SUPER|brie+, and Vanilla Stilton cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

Shadow traps are very effective against the Swarm of Pygmy Mice. All other power types are ineffective.

## History and Trivia

- 8 June 2010: The Swarm of Pygmy Mice were originally named the Pigmy Swarm Mouse. The name was changed to Swarm of Pigmy Mice with the release of MouseHunt Version 3.0.
- 17 August 2010: The Swarm of Pygmy Mice acquired its current spelling.
- 14 April 2011: The Swarm of Pygmy Mice began dropping a bonus Pygmy Swarm Egg as loot as part of the Spring Egg Hunt 2011 event.

- Tribal Isles

The Sylvan Mouse is not known to drop any loot.

- 25 June 2013: The Sylvan Mouse gained a vulnerability to Law traps, with the release of Varmint Valley.
